([Sloosmore/outcomes-optimizer](https://github.com/Sloosmore/outcomes-optimizer/tree/HEAD/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Set up a path through the plan for your role, shown in the dashboard. Offer it when someone says they are new or need to get up to speed; start it only on a yes. For one fact use /vela:find. ([smalls257/vela](https://github.com/smalls257/vela/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Interactive wizard that scaffolds a new agent or skill with correct frontmatter, a rubric skeleton, and directory layout. ([SnakeDawg/brainstorming](https://github.com/SnakeDawg/brainstorming/tree/HEAD/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: One-time setup for the Surface loop — create (or adopt) your vault, write the config, seed the style and rubric templates, and explain how the loop works. Run "/onboard" once after installing the surface plugin, or again to repair/relocate a vault. ([sonnytaite/surface-plugin](https://github.com/sonnytaite/surface-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Onboard a website onto this growth kit, or re-check an existing setup. Walks stage A of the flow (the setup steps `growth doctor` lists), using `growth doctor` to see what is done, asks the owner only what is still open, records every answer, and stops when doctor's last line says every step is done (the count is whatever doctor prints; it grows with the kit). Use on a new project, when the user asks to set up, check, connect or re-run onboarding, or at the start of a session when doctor is not green. ([spcaeo/growth-ops-kit](https://github.com/spcaeo/growth-ops-kit/tree/HEAD/kit/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Bring a project under orchestrator coordination -- inventory existing repos (brownfield), scaffold a fresh project (greenfield), or draft a phased integration plan. Use when a human wants to start coordinating a new set of repos, or refresh an existing project's inventory/plan. ([specfuse/orchestrator](https://github.com/specfuse/orchestrator/tree/HEAD/plugins/specfuse-orchestrator/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Onboard an existing git project with Jira sync — imports full commit history into Jira cards and configures automatic sync. Use on existing repos that need Jira integration. ([spsantos03/jira-project-sync](https://github.com/spsantos03/jira-project-sync/tree/HEAD/plugins/jira-project-sync/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Run the group's kickoff — the conversation that produces PLAN.md, and the checks that confirm a clone is set up right. Use when a student runs /onboard, when PLAN.md does not exist yet, when a write is blocked because the group's plan is not settled, or when someone has just cloned the fork. ([StephanUllmann/task-harness-run-2](https://github.com/StephanUllmann/task-harness-run-2/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Produce a guided tour of this codebase - architecture, main flows, where things live, how to run and test, and gotchas. Use when new to a repo or returning after a long break. ([sumsam-ali-sac/jibe](https://github.com/sumsam-ali-sac/jibe/tree/HEAD/template/.claude/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Sets up one person on one machine for this team repo - writes .local/me.yaml, creates their people/ folder, grants the agent access to their project clones and checks tools. Use when .local/me.yaml is missing, or the user says "onboard me", "set me up", "I'm new to the team", "new laptop", "configure my machine" or "why can't you see my repos". Not for setting up a new team repo (use setup). ([sumsolution/folkways](https://github.com/sumsolution/folkways/tree/HEAD/packages/content/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: First-impression onboarding conversation for Corinne. Guides her through personality setup, goal capture, and ChatGPT import in a natural, conversational flow. ([Supernor/openclaw-skills](https://github.com/Supernor/openclaw-skills/tree/HEAD/eoin/onboard))
- **onboard**: The place to start in any project. In a codebase, it maps the layout, conventions, how to run and test it, and the existing patterns for common jobs, writes them to .senior/repo-map.md, and on the first day asks the user which choices already in the code were deliberate, so the ledger starts with real decisions. In an empty folder, it starts the design session instead. Use once on a new or large codebase, and again only when the user asks for a refresh. ([SwagatPatel03/senior-beside-you](https://github.com/SwagatPatel03/senior-beside-you/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Use when the user runs /qa:onboard or asks to set a project up for the QA department for the first time. Not for testing a work item, and not for changing a profile that already exists. ([syedmuhdahmad/Alchemist-QA](https://github.com/syedmuhdahmad/Alchemist-QA/tree/HEAD/plugins/qa/skills/onboard))
- **onboard**: Взять проект под кит: завести базу знаний, связать с ней основную копию, завести место оператора — его папку в базе и личный репозиторий — и заполнить базу разговором с оператором. Для каталога, который под китом ещё не числится, — пустой папки нового проекта или существующего репозитория, и для машины, где оператор базы ещё не назван. Звать словами «новый проект», «поставь кит на этот проект», «свяжи с базой знаний» или скиллом onboard. Не для ведения работы — это drive, и не для ремонта разорванной связи. ([t8nax/agents-kit](https://github.com/t8nax/agents-kit/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/onboard))
